(112)

\displaystyle\{L_{i}[\beta_{1}^{i}],L_{j}[\beta_{2}^{j}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-G_{k}\left[\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}\beta_{1}^{i}\beta_{2}^{j}\right]\,.

(113)

The vector constraint commutes with the Lorentz–Gauss constraints,

\displaystyle\{H_{c}[N^{c}],G_{k}[\theta^{k}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(114)

\displaystyle\{H_{c}[N^{c}],L_{k}[\beta^{k}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(115)

while its bracket with itself yields

\displaystyle\{H_{a}[N^{a}],H_{c}[\epsilon^{c}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-H_{a}\left[\mathcal{L}_{\vec{\epsilon}}N^{a}\right]-L_{i}\left[\epsilon^{a}N^{b}F^{0i}_{ab}\right]

(116)

\displaystyle\!\!-G_{i}\left[\epsilon^{a}N^{b}{\cal F}^{i}_{ab}\right]\,.

This last bracket suggests that the linear combination
\mathfrak{D}_{a}[N^{a}]=H_{a}[N^{a}]+G_{i}[\Gamma_{a}^{i}N^{a}]+L_{i}[K_{a}^{i}N^{a}]
generates spatial diffeomorphisms.
Indeed, such generator can be written as

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}_{a}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!{\cal P}^{b}_{i}\partial_{a}A_{b}^{i}+{\cal K}^{b}_{i}\partial_{a}B_{b}^{i}-\partial_{b}\left({\cal P}^{b}_{i}A_{a}^{i}+{\cal K}^{b}_{i}B_{a}^{i}\right)

(117)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!{\cal P}^{b}_{i}\partial_{a}{\cal D}_{b}^{i}+v^{i}\partial_{a}{\cal E}_{i}+\mathfrak{K}_{ij}\partial_{a}{\cal B}^{ij}-\partial_{b}\left({\cal P}^{b}_{i}{\cal D}_{a}^{i}\right)\,,

and, therefore, it generates spatial diffeomorphisms along the shift,

\displaystyle\{A_{a}^{i},\mathfrak{D}_{b}[N^{b}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\mathcal{L}_{\vec{N}}A_{a}^{i}\,,

(118)

\displaystyle\{B_{a}^{i},\mathfrak{D}_{b}[N^{b}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\mathcal{L}_{\vec{N}}B_{a}^{i}\,,

(119)

\displaystyle\{{\cal P}^{a}_{i},\mathfrak{D}_{b}[N^{b}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\mathcal{L}_{\vec{N}}{\cal P}^{a}_{i}\,,

(120)

\displaystyle\{{\cal K}^{a}_{i},\mathfrak{D}_{b}[N^{b}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\mathcal{L}_{\vec{N}}{\cal K}^{a}_{i}\,,

(121)

on and off the second-class constraint surface for all phase-space variables.
It follows that

\displaystyle\{{\cal O},H_{b}[N^{b}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\mathcal{L}_{\vec{N}}{\cal O}+\{G_{i}\left[\Gamma_{b}^{i}N^{b}\right],{\cal O}\}

(122)

\displaystyle\!\!+\{L_{i}\left[K_{b}^{i}N^{b}\right],{\cal O}\}\,,

for an arbitrary phase-space function
{\cal O}
.

Because the Lorentz–Gauss constraints generate proper Lorentz transformations of
e^{a}_{I}
and
F^{IJ}_{ab}
even off shell, it follows from the Lorentz invariance of
\frac{|e|}{16\pi G}e^{a}_{I}e^{b}_{J}\mathchoice{P^{{{I}{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}{L}}}}{P^{{{I}{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}{L}}}}{P^{{{I}{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}{L}}}}{P^{{{I}{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}{L}}}}F^{KL}_{ab}=-H[N]-H_{a}[N^{a}]
—using (
114
) and (
115
)—that they commute with the Hamiltonian constraint too:

\displaystyle\{H[N],G_{k}[\theta^{k}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(123)

\displaystyle\{H[N],L_{k}[\beta^{k}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,.

(124)

Use of (
122
) then implies the bracket

\displaystyle\{H[N],H_{c}[\epsilon^{c}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-H[\mathcal{L}_{\vec{\epsilon}}N]+G_{i}\left[N\epsilon^{a}{\cal F}^{i}_{\bar{0}a}\right]

(125)

\displaystyle\!\!+L_{i}\left[N\epsilon^{a}F^{0i}_{\bar{0}a}\right]\,,

where we defined the expressions

\displaystyle F_{\bar{0}a}^{0i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{1}{N}\{K_{a}^{i},H[N]\}\,,

\displaystyle{\cal F}_{\bar{0}a}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{1}{N}\{\Gamma_{a}^{i},H[N]\}\,,

(126)

which are compatible with the identification of the normal components

\displaystyle n^{\mu}F^{0i}_{\mu a}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{1}{N}F_{ta}^{0i}-\frac{N^{b}}{N}F_{ba}^{0i}=\frac{1}{N}\{K_{a}^{i},H[N]\}\,,

\displaystyle n^{\mu}{\cal F}_{\mu a}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{1}{N}{\cal F}_{ta}^{i}-\frac{N^{b}}{N}{\cal F}_{ba}^{i}=\frac{1}{N}\{\Gamma_{a}^{i},H[N]\}\,,

(127)

where we substituted
\dot{K}_{a}^{i}=\{K_{a}^{i},H[N]+H_{b}[N^{b}]+L_{j}[K_{t}^{j}]+G_{j}[\Gamma_{t}^{j}]\}
and
\dot{\Gamma}_{a}^{i}=\{K_{a}^{i},H[N]+H_{b}[N^{b}]+L_{j}[K_{t}^{j}]+G_{j}[\Gamma_{t}^{j}]\}
in the time components of the strength tensor.

Using all the above, we compute the brackets of the
H^{(1)}
and
H^{(2)}
components of the Hamiltonian constraint:

\displaystyle\{H^{(1)}[N],H^{(1)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}=0\,,

(128)

\displaystyle\!\!\{H^{(2)}[N],H^{(1)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}+\{H^{(1)}[N],H^{(2)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad=-H^{(2)}\left[\frac{\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}\gamma v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{q}(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\partial_{a}N-N\partial_{a}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})\right]

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\quad-H_{a}\left[q^{ab}\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\partial_{b}N-N\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\right)\right]\,,

(129)

where
q^{ab}
is the inverse spatial metric (
67
), and

\displaystyle\!\!\{H^{(2)}[N],H^{(2)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}

(130)

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad=H^{(2)}\left[\frac{\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}\gamma v^{q}{\cal P}^{b}_{q}\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\partial_{b}N-N\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\right)\right]\,.

Therefore, the full bracket of the Hamiltonian constraint with itself is given by

\{H[N],H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}=-H_{a}\left[q^{ab}\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\partial_{b}N-N\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\right)\right]\,.

(131)

In summary, the constraint algebra, using Poisson brackets in the extended phase space, is given by

\displaystyle\{H_{a}[N^{a}],H_{c}[\epsilon^{c}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-H_{a}\left[\mathcal{L}_{\vec{\epsilon}}N^{a}\right]-G_{i}\left[\epsilon^{a}N^{b}{\cal F}^{i}_{ab}\right]

(132)

\displaystyle\!\!-L_{i}\left[\epsilon^{a}N^{b}F^{0i}_{ab}\right]\,,

\displaystyle\{H[N],H_{c}[\epsilon^{c}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-H[\mathcal{L}_{\vec{\epsilon}}N]+G_{i}\left[N\epsilon^{a}{\cal F}^{i}_{\bar{0}a}\right]

(133)

\displaystyle\!\!+L_{i}\left[N\epsilon^{a}F^{0i}_{\bar{0}a}\right]\,,

\displaystyle\{H[N],H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-H_{a}\left[q^{ab}\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\partial_{b}N-N\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\right)\right]\,,\qquad

(134)

\displaystyle\{H_{c}[N^{c}],G_{k}[\theta^{k}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(135)

\displaystyle\{H_{c}[N^{c}],L_{k}[\beta^{k}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(136)

\displaystyle\{H[N],G_{k}[\theta^{k}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(137)

\displaystyle\{H[N],L_{k}[\beta^{k}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(138)

\displaystyle\{G_{i}[\Gamma_{t}^{i}],G_{j}[\theta^{j}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!G_{k}\left[\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}\Gamma_{t}^{i}\theta^{j}\right]\,,

(139)

\displaystyle\{G_{i}[\Gamma_{t}^{i}],L_{j}[\beta^{j}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!L_{k}\left[\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}\Gamma_{t}^{i}\beta^{j}\right]\,,

(140)

\displaystyle\{L_{i}[K_{t}^{i}],L_{j}[\beta^{j}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-G_{k}\left[\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}{j}}}}K_{t}^{i}\beta^{j}\right]\,.

(141)

This algebra is first class and is precisely what one would expect from an extrapolation of the Einstein–Yang–Mills system’s algebra (
7
)-(
11
).

V.4
Gauge transformations and covariance

We say that the spacetime is covariant if the canonical gauge transformation is equivalent to a diffeomorphism of the spacetime metric,

\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}g_{\mu\nu}\big|_{\rm OS}=\mathcal{L}_{\xi}g_{\mu\nu}\big|_{\rm OS}\,,

(142)

where ”OS” indicates an evaluation on shell, involving not only the vanishing of the constraints but also use of Hamilton’s equations of motion on the right-hand side for the time-derivatives:
\dot{q}_{ab}=\{q_{ab},H[N,\vec{N}]\}
.
The gauge parameters
(\epsilon^{\bar{0}},\epsilon^{a})
in (
142
) are related to the four-vector field
\xi^{\mu}
that acts as the diffeomorphism generator by a change of basis from the observer’s frame to the Eulerian frame associated to the foliation:

\displaystyle\xi^{\mu}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\xi^{t}t^{\mu}+\xi^{a}s^{\mu}_{a}=\epsilon^{\bar{0}}n^{\mu}+\epsilon^{a}s^{\mu}_{a}\,,

\displaystyle\xi^{t}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}\quad,\quad\xi^{a}=\epsilon^{a}-\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}N^{a}\,.

(143)

The components of the spacetime metric in (
142
) are given by those of the ADM line element (
1
), which are expressed in terms of
N
,
N^{a}
, and
q_{ab}
.
Therefore, the left-hand side of (
142
) requires gauge transformations of
N
and
N^{a}
which are not directly provided by the Poisson brackets because they do not have momenta.
Instead, their gauge transformations, as well as those of
K_{t}^{i}
and
\Gamma_{t}^{i}
, are defined by the preservation of the form of Hamilton’s equations of motion, hence they are determined by the constraint algebra (
132
)-(
141
) and given by

\displaystyle\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}N\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\dot{\epsilon}^{0}+\epsilon^{b}\partial_{b}N-N^{b}\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\,,

(144)

\displaystyle\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}N^{a}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\dot{\epsilon}^{a}+\epsilon^{b}\partial_{b}N^{a}-N^{b}\partial_{b}\epsilon^{a}

(145)

\displaystyle\!\!+q^{ab}\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\partial_{b}N-N\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\right)\,,

\displaystyle\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}K_{t}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\dot{\beta}^{i}+\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}\left(\beta^{j}\Gamma_{t}^{k}+\theta^{j}K_{t}^{k}\right)+\epsilon^{a}N^{b}F^{0i}_{ab}

(146)

\displaystyle\!\!+\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}N^{a}-N\epsilon^{a}\right)F^{0i}_{\bar{0}a}\,,

\displaystyle\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}\Gamma_{t}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\dot{\theta}^{i}+\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}\left(\theta^{j}\Gamma_{t}^{k}-\beta^{j}K_{t}^{k}\right)+\epsilon^{a}N^{b}{\cal F}^{i}_{ab}

(147)

\displaystyle\!\!+\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}N^{a}-N\epsilon^{a}\right){\cal F}^{i}_{\bar{0}a}\,.

The transformations (
144
) and (
145
) correspond precisely to spacetime diffeomorphisms of the lapse and shift,
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}N
and
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}N^{a}
, respectively.
On the other hand, the canonical decomposition of spacetime diffeomorphisms and proper Lorentz transformations—the latter denoted by
\delta^{\rm SO(1,3)}_{\theta,\beta}
in what follows—of the connection components are respectively given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}K_{t}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\delta^{\rm SO(1,3)}_{\xi^{\mu}\Gamma_{\mu},\xi^{\mu}K_{\mu}}K_{t}^{i}+\epsilon^{a}N^{b}F_{ab}^{0i}

(148)

\displaystyle\!\!+\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}N^{a}-N\epsilon^{a}\right)F_{0a}^{0i}\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\Gamma_{t}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\delta^{\rm SO(1,3)}_{\xi^{\mu}\Gamma_{\mu},\xi^{\mu}K_{\mu}}\Gamma_{t}^{i}+\epsilon^{a}N^{b}{\cal F}_{ab}^{i}

(149)

\displaystyle\!\!+\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}N^{a}-N\epsilon^{a}\right){\cal F}_{0a}^{i}\,,

and

\displaystyle\delta^{\rm SO(1,3)}_{\theta,\beta}K_{t}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\dot{\beta}^{i}+\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[ (151)

(See App.
A.6
and
A.7
for detailed derivations.)
A comparison with the canonical gauge transformations (
144
)-(
147
) reveals that the latter correspond to linear combinations of diffeomorphisms and Lorentz transformations for the Lagrange multipliers; specifically,

\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}=\mathcal{L}_{\xi}+\delta^{\rm SO(1,3)}_{\theta-\xi^{\mu}\Gamma_{\mu},\beta-\xi^{\mu}K_{\mu}}\,.

(152)

For the full system to be covariant, this transformation must hold for the full expressions of all physical tensor fields.

As expected, the lapse, the shift, and the structure function, and hence the line element, are invariant to Lorentz transformations,

\displaystyle\{q^{ab},G_{i}[\theta^{i}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(153)

\displaystyle\{q^{ab},L_{i}[\beta^{i}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,.

(154)

This, in turn, implies that the vector constraint does generate spatial diffeomorphisms of the spatial metric on shell:

\displaystyle\{q^{ab}(x),H_{c}[\epsilon^{c}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\mathcal{L}_{\vec{\epsilon}}\,q^{ab}-L_{i}\left[\{q^{ab}(x),K_{b}^{i}N^{b}\}\right]

(155)

\displaystyle\!\!-G_{i}\left[\{q^{ab}(x),\Gamma_{b}^{i}N^{b}\}\right]\,.

Furthermore, its canonical gauge transformation does not contain derivatives of the normal gauge function—see Appendix
A.8
for the detailed computation—

\frac{\partial\{q^{ab},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{c_{1}}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}=\frac{\partial\{q^{ab},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{c_{1}}\partial_{c_{2}}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}=\dotsi=0\,,

(156)

a necessary condition for (
142
) to hold that is beyond the requirement that the algebra be first class
EMGCov
.

Similarly, use of (
144
)-(
147
) and our previous observations that the Lorentz–Gauss constraints generate proper Lorentz transformations of the spatial components, as well as that the vector constraint generates the appropriate linear combination of spatial diffeomorphisms and SO(1,3) transformations, imply that the covariance condition of the tetrad

\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}e_{\mu}^{I}\big|_{\rm OS}=\mathcal{L}_{\xi}e_{\mu}^{I}+\delta^{\rm SO(1,3)}_{\theta-\xi^{\mu}\Gamma_{\mu},\beta-\xi^{\mu}K_{\mu}}e_{\mu}^{I}\big|_{\rm OS}\,,

(157)

reduces to the conditions

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\{v_{i},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{c}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\gamma\sqrt{8\pi G}}{\cal P}^{c}_{i}\bigg|_{\rm OS}\,,

(158)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\{v_{i},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial((\partial_{c_{1}}\partial_{c_{2}}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(159)

and

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\delta^{a}_{c}\delta^{k}_{i}-\frac{1}{2}{\cal P}^{a}_{i}({\cal P}^{-1})^{k}_{c}\right)\frac{\partial\{{\cal P}^{c}_{k},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{d}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad=-\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{j}{\cal P}^{a}_{j}{\cal P}^{d}_{i}\bigg|_{\rm OS}\,,

(160)

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\delta^{a}_{c}\delta^{k}_{i}-\frac{1}{2}{\cal P}^{a}_{i}({\cal P}^{-1})^{k}_{c}\right)\frac{\partial\{{\cal P}^{c}_{k},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{d_{1}}\partial_{d_{2}}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}=0\,,\qquad

(161)

while the covariance condition of the connection

OS}=\dotsi=0\,.

(163)

It can be readily verified that the covariance condition of the connection (
163
) holds because the Hamiltonian constraint has no derivatives of the momenta.
On the other hand, it requires more work to evaluate the covariance conditions of the tetrad (
158
)-(
161
), but they indeed hold.
See Appendix
A.8
for a detailed proof.

Therefore, the full canonical gauge transformations of the spacetime metric, the tetrad, and the connection indeed correspond to linear combinations of spacetime diffeomorphisms and SO(1,3) transformations; all geometric objects constructed from these, such as curvature or torsion tensors, then transform in a corresponding manner; hence the theory is indeed fully covariant in its canonical formulation.
This is true on and off the second-class constraint surface: As we show in the next section, use of Dirac brackets to impose the second-class constraints preserves the above outcome.

V.5
Dirac observables

The Lorentz–Gauss constraints, being first class, imply that the functionals

\displaystyle\mathfrak{L}_{i}[\alpha^{i}]\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\int{\rm (164)

the densitized four-currents
{\cal L}_{i}^{\mu}=({\cal L}_{i}^{t},{\cal L}_{i}^{a})
and
{\cal G}_{i}^{\mu}=({\cal G}_{i}^{t},{\cal G}_{i}^{a})
are conserved because the brackets (
168
)-(
173
) imply
\dot{\cal L}_{i}^{t}=-\partial_{a}{\cal L}_{i}^{a}
and
\dot{\cal G}_{i}^{t}=-\partial_{a}{\cal G}_{i}^{a}
on shell, and hence
\partial_{\mu}{\cal L}_{i}^{\mu}=\nabla_{\mu}{\cal L}_{i}^{\mu}=0
and
\partial_{\mu}{\cal G}_{i}^{\mu}=\nabla_{\mu}{\cal G}_{i}^{\mu}=0
.

Moreover, these observables form a local Lorentz algebra,

\displaystyle\{\mathfrak{G}_{i}(x),\mathfrak{G}_{j}(y)\}\!\!

VI
Second-class constraints

VI.1
Primary and secondary second-class constraints

We now proceed to obtain the contribution of each first-class constraint to the second-class constraint
{\cal C}_{ij}
.
First, the Lorentz–Gauss constraints do not contribute on the second-class constraint surface:

\displaystyle{\cal C}_{ij}^{(G)}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-\frac{\delta G_{k}[\Gamma_{t}^{k}]}{\delta{\cal B}^{ij}}

(179)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-2\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{p}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.59366pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.56691pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}{(i}}}}{\epsilon^{{{p}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.59366pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.56691pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}{(i}}}}{\epsilon^{{{p}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.59366pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.56691pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}{(i}}}}{\epsilon^{{{p}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.59366pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.56691pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}{(i}}}}\delta^{q}_{j)}\Gamma_{t}^{m}\mathfrak{K}_{pq}\,,

\displaystyle{\cal C}_{ij}^{(L)}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-\frac{\delta L_{k}[K_{t}^{k}]}{\delta{\cal B}^{ij}}

(180)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-4\delta^{p}_{(i}\delta_{j)[m}v_{q]}K_{t}^{m}\mathfrak{K}_{p}^{q}\,.

Using (
117
), we obtain the vector constraint contribution

\displaystyle{\cal C}_{ij}^{(V)}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-\frac{\delta H_{a}[N^{a}]}{\delta{\cal B}^{ij}}

(181)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-\frac{\delta\mathfrak{D}_{a}[N^{a}]}{\delta{\cal B}^{ij}}-\frac{\delta G_{k}[\Gamma_{a}^{k}N^{a}]}{\delta{\cal B}^{ij}}-\frac{\delta L_{k}[K_{a}^{k}N^{a}]}{\delta{\cal B}^{ij}}

\displaystyle=

When all the first-class constraints vanish and
\mathfrak{K}_{ij}=0
, this vanishes identically and hence neither do
H_{a}
or
H^{(1)}
contribute to
{\cal C}_{ij}
on shell.

Therefore, the only nontrivial contribution comes from the second term of the Hamiltonian constraint,

\displaystyle{\cal C}_{ij}^{(2)}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-\frac{\delta H^{(2)}[N]}{\delta{\cal B}^{ij}}

(182)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-N\frac{\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\gamma\sqrt{8\pi G}}{\cal T}_{ij}\,,

where
{\cal T}^{ij}
is given precisely by the torsion components (
70
).
This can be written as

(188)

Because we are able to solve the second-class constraints, we do not need Dirac brackets, but only to substitute this solution into the Hamiltonian constraint, obtaining the reduced expression

B}^{p}_{m}\bar{\cal B}^{q}_{n}\Bigg)\Bigg]\,,

and similarly for the other constraints.
We can then work entirely in the reduced phase space spanned by the canonical pairs
({\cal P},{\cal D})
and
(v,{\cal E})
.
However, the reduced constraints are much less symmetric than in its extended phase-space version, complicating the explicit computation of the brackets.
It is more convenient to work in the extended phase space and instead use Dirac brackets to preserve the second-class constraints under time evolution or gauge transformations as we show below.

VI.2
Dirac brackets

To formulate the Dirac brackets we first compute the Poisson brackets between the second-class constraints:

\{\mathfrak{K}_{ij}(x),\mathfrak{K}^{kl}(y)\}=0\,,

(190)

\{\mathfrak{K}_{ij}(x),{\cal (200)

If
{\cal U}
is a first-class constraint, then the correction bracket
\{{\cal O},{\cal U}\}_{\rm C}
is nontrivial on shell—on the first-class and second-class constraint surfaces—only if
{\cal O}
depends on
{\cal B}_{ij}
.
If
{\cal O}
too is a first-class constraint, then the correction bracket is proportional to first-class and second-class constraints, and hence the set of constraints given by
G_{i}
,
L_{i}
,
H_{a}
, and
H
remains first class on the second-class constraint surface when using Dirac brackets.
Furthermore, the above implies that the correction brackets cannot contribute to the covariance conditions of the metric (
156
) and the tetrad (
158
)-(
161
), and it can contribute to the covariance condition of the connection (
163
) only via its dependence on
{\cal B}_{ij}
.
However, the only possible contribution for the latter is proportional to

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\{{\cal T}_{ij},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{c}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(201)

which vanishes on shell because
{\cal T}_{ij}
is related to the torsion tensor by (
70
):
As a function of the connection and the tetrad, the torsion tensor automatically satisfies the covariance equation

\delta_{\epsilon,\theta,\beta}T^{I}_{\mu\nu}\big|_{\rm OS}=\mathcal{L}_{\xi}T^{I}_{\mu\nu}+\delta^{\rm SO(1,3)}_{\theta-\xi^{\mu}\Gamma_{\mu},\beta-\xi^{\mu}K_{\mu}}T^{I}_{\mu\nu}\big|_{\rm OS}

(202)

as generated by the first-class constraints with Poisson brackets because the tetrad and the connection satisfy their own covariance conditions in the same context; in particular, the normal part of this equation for the relevant spatial components implies

\frac{\partial\{T_{ab}^{i},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{d}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}=-2T_{\bar{0}[a}^{i}\delta_{b]}^{d}\bigg|_{\rm OS}\,,

(203)

while the transformation of the rest of the phase-space variables in (
70
) result in terms proportional to
T^{k}_{ab}
; therefore, both terms vanish on shell if the second-class constraints are imposed—having a fully vanishing torsion—and hence all contributions to (
201
) vanish.
Therefore, all the covariance conditions hold using the Dirac bracket too.

Finally, the Dirac observables (
164
) and (
165
) still commute with the first-class constraints using Dirac brackets because they are independent of
{\cal B}_{ij}
on the second-class constraint surface.

VII
Cosmological constant

A cosmological constant can be easily incorporated into the previous analyses.
Its contribution to the action is given by the addition of

\displaystyle S_{\Lambda}[e,\omega]\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\int{\rm d}^{4}x\;\frac{|\det e|}{16\pi G}\left[-2\Lambda\right]

(204)

to (
18
).
This implies a contribution to the Hamiltonian constraint (
104
) given by the addition of

H_{\Lambda}=\frac{\sqrt{\det{\cal P}}}{\gamma}\sqrt{8\pi G}\Lambda\,.

(205)

As previously shown,
\sqrt{\det{\cal P}}/\gamma
is invariant to Lorentz transformations and hence

\displaystyle\{H_{\Lambda}[N],G_{i}[\theta^{i}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(206)

\displaystyle\{H_{\Lambda}[N],L_{i}[\beta^{i}]\}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,.

(207)

This, in turn, implies that
\{\sqrt{\det{\cal P}}/\gamma,H_{a}[\epsilon^{a}]\}=\mathcal{L}_{\vec{\epsilon}}\left(\sqrt{\det{\cal P}}/\gamma\right)
and hence

\{H_{\Lambda}[N],H_{a}[\epsilon^{a}]\}=-H_{\Lambda}[\epsilon^{a}\partial_{a}N]\,.

(208)

Finally, using

\frac{\partial\{\sqrt{\det{\cal P}}/\gamma,H^{(1)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]+H^{(2)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{d}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}=0\,,

(209)

we obtain

\displaystyle\!\!\{H_{\Lambda}[N],H^{(1)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]+H^{(2)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}

(210)

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\qquad+\{H^{(1)}[N]+H^{(2)}[N],H_{\Lambda}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}=0\,.

All the above, together with

\{H_{\Lambda}[N],H_{\Lambda}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}=0\,,

(211)

implies that the algebra (
132
)-(
141
) is preserved when
H_{\Lambda}
is incorporated into the Hamiltonian constraint
H=H^{(1)}+H^{(2)}+H_{\Lambda}
.
Furthermore, because
H_{\Lambda}
contains no spatial derivatives, it does not contribute to any of the covariance conditions.
It does not contribute to the second-class constraints either because it does not depend on
{\cal B}_{ij}
.
Finally, its addition preserves the commutation of the Dirac observables (
164
) and (
165
) with the first-class constraints.

VIII
Geometry and canonical quantization

VIII.1
Area and volume functionals

The tetrads
e^{\mu}_{I}
constitute four orthonormal vectors—one timelike (
I=0
) and the three spacelike (
I=i=1,2,3
)—and represent a valid dynamical frame (subject to transformations generated by the first-class constraints), such that they may be used to compute a geometric area (internal-)2-form of a two-dimensional surface
{\cal S}
of the spacetime manifold,

\displaystyle{\cal A}_{IJ}[{\cal S}]=-\int_{\cal S}{\rm d}^{2}s\,\epsilon_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}e^{\mu}_{I}e^{\nu}_{J}w_{1}^{\alpha}w_{2}^{\beta}\,,

(212)

where
w_{1}
and
w_{2}
coordinatize
{\cal S}
, and

w_{1}^{\alpha}=\frac{\partial x^{\alpha}}{\partial w_{1}}\quad,\quad w_{2}^{\beta}=\frac{\partial x^{\beta}}{\partial w_{2}}\,.

(213)

Consider a coordinate system
x^{\mu}=(t,x,y,z)
and a spacelike surface
{\cal S}
such that
w_{a}=\mathchoice{\varepsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{a}{b}{c}}}}{\varepsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{a}{b}{c}}}}{\varepsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{a}{b}{c}}}}{\varepsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scr
