On the other hand, the tetrad has the simpler transformation

\displaystyle\delta^{\rm SO(1,3)}_{\theta,\beta}e^{\mu}_{I}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-\mathchoice{\Omega^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}}{\Omega^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}}{\Omega^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}}{\Omega^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}}e^{\mu}_{J}

(297)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\delta^{0}_{I}e^{\mu}_{i}-\delta_{Ii}e^{\mu}_{0}\right)\beta^{i}-\delta_{Ii}e^{\mu}_{j}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}\theta^{k}\,.

A.7
Lie derivatives

The Lie derivative of the connection 1-form

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\omega_{\mu}^{IJ}=\xi^{\nu}\partial_{\nu}\omega_{\mu}^{IJ}+\omega_{\nu}^{IJ}\partial_{\mu}\xi^{\nu}\,,

(298)

generated by the four-vector
\xi^{\mu}
, can be written in terms of the gauge functions
(\epsilon^{\bar{0}},\epsilon^{a})
using (
143
),

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\omega_{t}^{IJ}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\xi^{t}\omega_{t}^{IJ}\right)^{\bullet}+\xi^{a}\partial_{a}\omega_{t}^{IJ}+\omega_{a}^{IJ}\dot{\xi}^{a}

(299)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\xi^{\mu}\omega_{\mu}^{IJ}\right)^{\bullet}+\left(\epsilon^{a}-\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}N^{a}\right)\left(\partial_{a}\omega_{t}^{IJ}-\dot{\omega}_{a}^{IJ}\right)\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\omega_{a}^{IJ}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}\left(\dot{\omega}_{a}^{IJ}-\mathcal{L}_{\vec{N}}\omega_{a}^{IJ}\right)+\left(\omega_{t}^{IJ}-N^{b}\omega_{b}^{IJ}\right)\partial_{a}\xi^{t}

(300)

\displaystyle\!\!+\mathcal{L}_{\vec{\epsilon}}\omega_{a}^{IJ}\,.

From this we derive the Lie derivatives in terms of relevant canonical variables and Lorentz transformations:

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}K_{t}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\xi^{t}K_{t}^{i}\right)^{\bullet}+\xi^{a}\partial_{a}K_{t}^{i}+K^{i}_{a}\dot{\xi}^{a}

(301)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\xi^{t}K_{t}^{i}+\xi^{a}K^{i}_{a}\right)^{\bullet}+\xi^{a}\left(\partial_{a}K_{t}^{i}-\dot{K}_{a}^{i}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\xi^{\mu}K_{\mu}^{i}\right)^{\bullet}-\xi^{a}\left(F_{ta}^{0i}-\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}\left(\Gamma_{a}^{j}K_{t}^{k}+K_{a}^{j}\Gamma_{t}^{k}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\xi^{\mu}K_{\mu}^{i}\right)^{\bullet}+\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}\left(\xi^{\mu}K_{\mu}^{j}\Gamma_{t}^{k}+\xi^{\mu}\Gamma_{\mu}^{j}K_{t}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle\!\!+\epsilon^{a}N^{b}F_{ab}^{0i}+\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}N^{a}-N\epsilon^{a}\right)F_{\bar{0}a}^{0i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\delta^{\rm SO(1,3)}_{\xi^{\mu}\Gamma_{\mu},\xi^{\mu}K_{\mu}}K_{t}^{i}+\epsilon^{a}N^{b}F_{ab}^{0i}+\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}N^{a}-N\epsilon^{a}\right)F_{\bar{0}a}^{0i}\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\Gamma_{t}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\xi^{t}\Gamma_{t}^{i}\right)^{\bullet}+\xi^{a}\partial_{a}\Gamma_{t}^{i}+\Gamma_{a}^{i}\dot{\xi}^{a}

(302)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\xi^{\mu}\Gamma_{\mu}^{i}\right)^{\bullet}+\xi^{a}\left(\partial_{a}\Gamma_{t}^{i}-\dot{\Gamma}_{a}^{i}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\xi^{\mu}\Gamma_{\mu}^{i}\right)^{\bullet}+\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}\left(\xi^{\mu}\Gamma_{\mu}^{j}\Gamma_{t}^{k}-\xi^{\mu}K_{\mu}^{j}K_{t}^{k}\right)-\xi^{a}{\cal F}_{ta}^{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\delta^{\rm SO(1,3)}_{\xi^{\mu}\Gamma_{\mu},\xi^{\mu}K_{\mu}}\Gamma_{t}^{i}+\epsilon^{a}N^{b}{\cal F}_{ab}^{i}+\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}N^{a}-N\epsilon^{a}\right){\cal F}_{\bar{0}a}^{i}

for the Lagrange multipliers and

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}K^{i}_{a}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}\left(\dot{K}_{a}^{i}-\mathcal{L}_{\vec{N}}K^{i}_{a}\right)+\left(K_{t}^{i}-N^{b}K^{i}_{b}\right)\partial_{a}\xi^{t}

(303)

\displaystyle\!\!+\mathcal{L}_{\vec{\epsilon}}K^{i}_{a}\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\Gamma^{i}_{a}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}\left(\dot{\Gamma}_{a}^{i}-\mathcal{L}_{\vec{N}}\Gamma^{i}_{a}\right)+\left(\Gamma_{t}^{i}-N^{b}\Gamma^{i}_{b}\right)\partial_{a}\xi^{t}

(304)

\displaystyle\!\!+\mathcal{L}_{\vec{\epsilon}}\Gamma^{i}_{a}

for the configuration variables.

Similarly, the Lie derivative of the tetrad is given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}e^{t}_{I}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}\dot{e}^{t}_{I}+\left(\epsilon^{b}-\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}N^{b}\right)\partial_{b}e^{t}_{I}

(305)

\displaystyle\!\!-\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}e^{t}_{I}\left(\frac{\dot{\epsilon}^{0}}{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}-\frac{\dot{N}}{N}\right)-\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}e^{b}_{I}\left(\frac{\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}-\frac{\partial_{b}N}{N}\right)\,,

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\left(e^{a}_{I}+N^{a}e^{t}_{I}\right)\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}\left(e^{a}_{I}+N^{a}e^{t}_{I}\right)^{\bullet}

(306)

\displaystyle\!\!+e^{t}_{I}q^{ab}\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\partial_{b}N-N\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\right)

\displaystyle\!\!-\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}\mathcal{L}_{\vec{N}}\left(e^{a}_{I}+N^{a}e^{t}_{I}\right)

\displaystyle\!\!+\mathcal{L}_{\vec{\epsilon}}\left(e^{a}_{I}+N^{a}e^{t}_{I}\right)\,.

And the Lie derivative of the co-tetrad is given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}e_{t}^{0}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\gamma\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\right)^{\bullet}+v_{i}\varepsilon_{a}^{i}\dot{\epsilon}^{a}+\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}N^{a}\left(v_{i}\varepsilon_{a}^{i}\right)^{\bullet}

\displaystyle\!\!+\left(\epsilon^{a}-\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}N^{a}\right)\partial_{a}\left(\gamma N+v_{j}N^{b}\varepsilon_{b}^{j}\right)\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}e_{t}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\gamma v^{i}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\right)^{\bullet}+\varepsilon^{i}_{a}\dot{\epsilon}^{a}+\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}N^{a}\dot{\varepsilon}^{i}_{a}

\displaystyle\!\!+\left(\epsilon^{a}-\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}N^{a}\right)\partial_{a}\left(\gamma Nv^{i}+N^{b}\varepsilon_{b}^{i}\right)

for the time components and

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}e_{a}^{0}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}\left(v_{i}\varepsilon_{a}^{i}\right)^{\bullet}+\gamma\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\left(\frac{\partial_{a}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}-\frac{\partial_{a}N}{N}\right)

\displaystyle\!\!-\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}\mathcal{L}_{\vec{N}}\left(v_{i}\varepsilon_{a}^{i}\right)+\mathcal{L}_{\vec{\epsilon}}\,\left(v_{i}\varepsilon_{a}^{i}\right)\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}e_{a}^{i}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}\dot{\varepsilon}_{a}^{i}+\gamma v^{i}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\left(\frac{\partial_{a}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}-\frac{\partial_{a}N}{N}\right)

\displaystyle\!\!-\frac{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{N}\mathcal{L}_{\vec{N}}\varepsilon_{a}^{i}+\mathcal{L}_{\vec{\epsilon}}\,\varepsilon_{a}^{i}

for the spatial components.

A.8
Covariance conditions

Using

\frac{\partial(\gamma^{2}v^{(m}v^{n)})}{\partial v^{k}}=2\gamma^{2}v^{(m}\left(\delta^{n)}_{k}+\gamma^{2}v^{n)}v_{k}\right)\,,

(307)

we obtain the relevant derivatives of the inverse spatial metric with respect to the phase-space variables:

\displaystyle\frac{\partial q^{ab}}{\partial v^{k}}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}{8\pi G}{\cal P}^{a}_{m}{\cal P}^{b}_{n}\frac{\partial(\gamma^{2}v^{(m}v^{n)})}{\partial v^{k}}

(308)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!2\gamma^{2}\frac{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}{8\pi G}\left(v^{m}{\cal P}^{(a}_{m}{\cal P}^{b)}_{k}+{\cal P}^{a}_{m}{\cal P}^{b}_{n}\gamma^{2}v^{m}v^{n}v_{k}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!2\gamma^{2}q^{ab}

\displaystyle\!\!+2\gamma^{2}\frac{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}{8\pi G}\left(v^{m}{\cal P}^{(a}_{m}{\cal P}^{b)}_{k}-{\cal P}^{a}_{m}{\cal P}^{b}_{n}\delta^{mn}v_{k}\right)

as well as

\displaystyle\frac{{\rm d}q^{ab}}{{\rm d}{\cal P}^{c}_{i}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial q^{ab}}{\partial{\cal P}^{c}_{i}}+\frac{\partial q^{ab}}{\partial v^{k}}\frac{\partial v^{k}}{\partial{\cal P}^{c}_{i}}\,,

(309)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial q^{ab}}{\partial{\cal K}^{c}_{i}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial q^{ab}}{\partial v^{k}}\frac{\partial v^{k}}{\partial{\cal K}^{c}_{i}}\,.

(310)

Because the Hamiltonian constraint contains only first-order spatial derivatives, the bracket
\{q^{ab},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}
can contain at most first-order spatial derivatives of the gauge function
\epsilon^{\bar{0}}
.
Using

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\{{\cal P}^{b}_{j},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{d}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!2\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{[d}_{q}{\cal P}^{b]}_{j}\,,

(311)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\{{\cal K}^{b}_{j},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{d}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!2\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{[d}_{q}{\cal K}^{b]}_{j}

(312)

\displaystyle\!\!+\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}{\cal P}^{b}_{p}{\cal P}^{d}_{q}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{p}{q}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{p}{q}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{p}{q}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\epsilon^{{{p}{q}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}\,,

we get

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\{v_{q},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{d}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}{\cal P}^{d}_{q}\,,

(313)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\{\det{\cal P},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{d}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!2\frac{\gamma\sqrt{\det{\cal P}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{d}_{q}\,,

(314)

and hence

\frac{\partial\{\gamma,H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{d}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}=\frac{\gamma^{2}\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{d}_{q}\,,

(315)

\frac{\partial\{\gamma/\sqrt{\det{\cal P}},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{d}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}=0\,.

(316)

Using all the above, it follows that the relevant terms cancel out in the transformation of the structure function such that

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\{q^{ab},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{d}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(317)

on and off the second-class and first-class constraint surfaces.
Therefore, the metric’s covariance condition (
142
) indeed holds.

Similarly, the relevant part of the tetrad’s covariance condition (
157
) is given by the normal transformations.
The time components of this covariance condition reduce to the single equation

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{\{v_{i},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}-\frac{\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\gamma\sqrt{8\pi G}}{\cal P}^{b}_{i}\frac{\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}\bigg|_{\rm OS}

(318)

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\qquad\qquad=\frac{\{v_{i},H[N]\}}{N}-\frac{\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\gamma\sqrt{8\pi G}}{\cal P}^{b}_{i}\frac{\partial_{b}N}{N}\bigg|_{\rm OS},

and the spatial components reduce to

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\delta^{a}_{c}\delta^{k}_{i}-\frac{1}{2}{\cal P}^{a}_{i}({\cal P}^{-1})^{k}_{c}\right)\frac{\{{\cal P}^{c}_{k},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}

(319)

\displaystyle\!\!+\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{j}{\cal P}^{a}_{j}{\cal P}^{b}_{i}\frac{\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}}\bigg|_{\rm OS}

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\qquad\qquad=\left(\delta^{a}_{c}\delta^{k}_{i}-\frac{1}{2}{\cal P}^{a}_{i}({\cal P}^{-1})^{k}_{c}\right)\frac{\{{\cal P}^{c}_{k},H[N]\}}{N}

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{j}{\cal P}^{a}_{j}{\cal P}^{b}_{i}\frac{\partial_{b}N}{N}\bigg|_{\rm OS}\,.

Because these conditions must hold for arbitrary
\epsilon^{\bar{0}}
and
N
, they imply the equations

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\{v_{i},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{c}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\gamma\sqrt{8\pi G}}{\cal P}^{c}_{i}\bigg|_{\rm OS}\,,

(320)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\{v_{i},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{c_{1}}\partial_{c_{2}}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!0\,,

(321)

and

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\delta^{a}_{c}\delta^{k}_{i}-\frac{1}{2}{\cal P}^{a}_{i}({\cal P}^{-1})^{k}_{c}\right)\frac{\partial\{{\cal P}^{c}_{k},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{d}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad=-\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{j}{\cal P}^{a}_{j}{\cal P}^{d}_{i}\bigg|_{\rm OS}\,,

(322)

\displaystyle\!\!\left(\delta^{a}_{c}\delta^{k}_{i}-\frac{1}{2}{\cal P}^{a}_{i}({\cal P}^{-1})^{k}_{c}\right)\frac{\partial\{{\cal P}^{c}_{k},H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}}{\partial(\partial_{d_{1}}\partial_{d_{2}}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})}\bigg|_{\rm OS}=0\,.\qquad

(323)

Using (
313
) and (
311
) we conclude that the covariance condition of the tetrad (
157
) is indeed satisfied.

A_{b}^{l}\right)\Bigg]\,.

)-(

), the brackets (

)-(

) are straightforward to show.

)-(

) and (

)-(

), it follows that the Lorentz–Gauss constraints commute with the vector constraint, hence showing (

) and (

).

) and (

) and (

), the bracket of the vector constraint with itself yields

\displaystyle\!\!\{H_{a}[N^{a}],H_{c}[M^{c}]\}

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad=-H_{a}\left[\mathcal{L}_{\vec{M}}N^{a}\right]-\int{\rm d}^{3}xN^{a}M^{b}\bigg[F^{0i}_{ab}\partial_{c}\tilde{\cal P}^{c}_{i}

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\qquad\quad+\tilde{P}^{c}_{i}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\tex \displaystyle\!\!\qquad\quad+G_{i}[N^{a}M^{b}{\cal F}^{i}_{ab}]\,,

(357)

which shows (
116
).

To compute
\{H[N],H[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}
, we proceed in steps.
First, using the transformations generated by the vector constraint, we obtain

\displaystyle\!\!\{H^{(1)}[N],H^{(1)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad=\int{\rm d}^{3}xN\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{q}\{H_{a},H^{(1)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\quad+H_{a}\left[N\{\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{q},H^{(1)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}\right]

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad=-H_{a}\left[\frac{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}{8\pi G}\gamma^{2}v^{p}v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{p}{\cal P}^{b}_{q}\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\partial_{b}N-N\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\quad-H_{a}\left[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\{\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{q},H^{(1)}[N]\}\right]

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\quad+H_{a}\left[N\{\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{q},H^{(1)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}\right]

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\qquad=0\,.

(358)

and

\displaystyle\!\!\{H^{(2)}[N],H^{(1)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}

(359)

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad=H_{a}\left[\{H^{(2)}[N],\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{q}\}\right]

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\quad+\int{\rm d}^{3}y\;\{H^{(2)}[N],H_{a}\}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{q}

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad=-H^{(2)}\left[\mathcal{L}_{\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{q}}N\right]

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\quad-H_{a}\left[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\{\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{q},H^{(2)}[N]\}\right]

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\quad+G_{k}\left[\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{q}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\{\Gamma^{k}_{a},H^{(2)}[N]\}\right]

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\quad+L_{k}\left[\frac{\gamma\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{q}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\{K^{k}_{a},H^{(2)}[N]\}\right]\,.

It is useful to define the operation

\delta^{(2)}_{\epsilon^{\bar{0}},N}{\cal O}=\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\{{\cal O},H^{(2)}[N]\}-N\{{\cal O},H^{(2)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}\,.

(360)

Using the transformations of App.
B.3
, we obtain

\delta^{(2)}_{\epsilon^{\bar{0}},N}{\cal P}^{a}_{i}=0\,,

(361)

\delta^{(2)}_{\epsilon^{\bar{0}},N}{\cal K}^{b}_{n}=-\frac{\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}\frac{{\cal P}^{a}_{p}{\cal P}^{b}_{q}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{p}{q}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}}{\epsilon^{{{p}{q}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}}{\epsilon^{{{p}{q}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}}{\epsilon^{{{p}{q}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$ (362)

\delta^{(2)}_{\epsilon^{\bar{0}},N}v_{q}=\frac{\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}\frac{1}{\gamma}{\cal P}^{b}_{q}\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\partial_{b}N-N\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\right)\,,

(363)

and hence

\displaystyle\delta^{(2)}_{\epsilon^{\bar{0}},N}\gamma\!\!

\displaystyle=

(364)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}\gamma^{2}v^{q}{\cal P}^{b}_{q}\left(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\partial_{b}N-N\partial_{b}\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\right)\,.

\displaystyle\!\!\{H^{(2)}[N],H^{(1)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}+\{H^{(1)}[N],H^{(2)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}

(365)

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad=-H^{(2)}\left[\frac{\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}\gamma v^{q}{\cal P}^{a}_{q}(\epsilon^{\bar{0}}\partial_{a}N-N\partial_{a}\epsilon^{\bar{0}})\right]

and

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\!\qquad=H^{(2)}\left[-\frac{N}{\gamma}\{\gamma,H^{(2)}[\epsilon^{\bar{0}}]\}\right]

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\!\qquad\quad-\int{\rm d}^{4}x\;\frac{\sqrt{(\det{\cal P})^{-1}}}{\sqrt{8\pi G}}\frac{{\cal P}^{a}_{p}{\cal Combining all the brackets above results in the full bracket (

).

B.5

Relevant functional derivatives of the second-class constraint (
70
) are given by

\displaystyle\frac{\delta{\cal T}^{ij}(x)}{\delta{\cal P}^{a}_{r}(z)}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\bigg[\left(\delta^{ij}\delta^{r}_{q}-\delta^{r(i}\delta_{q}^{j)}\right)\Gamma_{a}^{q}+\delta_{m}^{(i}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{j)}{r}{q}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{j)}{r}{q}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{j)}{r}{q}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{j)}{r}{q}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$} (367)

\displaystyle\!\!\quad-\left({\cal P}^{-1}\right)_{a}^{m}\left({\cal P}^{-1}\right)_{c}^{r}\delta_{m}^{(i}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{j)}{p}{q}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{j)}{p}{q}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{j)}{p}{q}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{j)}{p}{q}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\tex P}^{d}_{p}\partial_{d}{\cal P}_{q}^{c}\bigg]\delta^{3}(x-z)

\displaystyle\!\!+\left({\cal P}^{-1}\right)_{a}^{m}\delta_{m}^{(i}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{j)}{p}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{j)}{p}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{j)}{p}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{j)}{p}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.83858pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.20389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.00278pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c] P}^{d}_{p}\frac{\partial\delta^{3}(x-z)}{\partial x^{d}}\,,

\frac{\delta{\cal T}^{ij}(x)}{\delta{\cal K}^{a}_{r}(z)}=-\delta^{r(i}\delta_{q}^{j)}K_{a}^{q}\delta^{3}(x-z)\,,

(368)

\frac{\delta{\cal T}_{ij}(x)}{\delta B^{r}_{a}(z)}=\frac{1}{1+\zeta^{2}}\left[\delta_{ij}{\cal P}^{a}_{r}-\delta_{r(i}\tilde{\cal P}^{a}_{j)}\right]\delta^{3}(x-z)\,,

(369)

\frac{\delta{\cal T}_{ij}(x)}{\delta A^{r}_{a}(z)}=\frac{1}{1+\zeta^{2}}\left[-\delta_{r(i}\tilde{\cal K}^{a}_{j)}-\zeta\delta_{ij}{\cal P}^{a}_{r}\right]\delta^{3}(x-z)\,,

(370)

\frac{\delta{\cal T}_{ij}(x)}{\delta K^{r}_{a}(z)}=\frac{1}{1+\zeta^{2}}\left[-\delta_{r(i}\tilde{\cal K}^{a}_{j)}-\zeta\delta_{r(i}\tilde{\cal P}^{a}_{j)}\right]\delta^{3}(x-z)\,,

(371)

and hence

\displaystyle\frac{\partial{\cal T}_{ij}}{\partial{\cal B}_{kl}}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\left({\cal P}^{-1}\right)^{n}_{b}\delta_{n(k}\frac{\partial{\cal T}_{ij}}{\partial B_{b}^{l)}}-\delta_{n(k}{\cal K}^{b}_{l)}\left({\cal P}^{-1}\right)^{r}_{b}\left({\cal P}^{-1}\right)^{n}_{c}\frac{\partial{\cal T}_{ij}}{\partial A_{c}^{r}}

(372)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{1}{1+\zeta^{2}}\Bigg[\delta_{ij}\delta^{kl}-\delta_{(i}^{k}\delta^{l}_{j)}+\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.59366pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.56691pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{p}{(k}}}_{{{(i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.54518pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.54518pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.61075pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2934pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.59366pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.56691pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{p}{(k}}}_{{{(i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.54518pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.54518pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.61075pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2934pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.59366pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.56691pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{p}{(k}}}_{{{(i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.54518pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.54518pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.61075pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2934pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.59366pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.56691pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{p}{(k}}}_{{{(i}\mathchoice{\makebox[ Using the above, we obtain

\displaystyle\int{\rm d}^{3}z\frac{\delta{\cal T}_{ij}(x)}{\delta A^{r}_{a}(z)}\frac{\delta{\cal T}^{kl}(y)}{\delta{\cal P}^{a}_{r}(z)}\!\!

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!-\frac{1}{1+\zeta^{2}}\Bigg[\delta_{r(i}\tilde{\cal K}^{a}_{j)}\left(\delta^{kl}\delta^{r}_{p}-\delta_{p}^{(k}\delta^{l)r}\right)\Gamma_{a}^{p}+\zeta\delta_{ij}\delta_{p}^{(k}\delta^{l)r}{\cal K}^{a}_{r}K_{a}^{p}

(373)

\displaystyle\!\!\qquad\qquad+\left(v^{s}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.77432pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.77432pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29686pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64063pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.59366pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.56691pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{(k}}}_{{{s}{(i}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.54518pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.54518pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.61075pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2934pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.77432pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.77432pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29686pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64063pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.59366pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.56691pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{(k}}}_{{{s}{(i}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.54518pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.54518pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.61075pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2934pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.77432pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.77432pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29686pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64063pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.59366pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.56691pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{(k}}}_{{{s}{(i}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.54518pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.54518pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.61075pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2934pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.77432pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.77432pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29686pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64063pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\ma P}^{-1}\right)_{c}^{r}{\cal P}^{d}_{p}\partial_{d}{\cal P}_{q}^{c}\Bigg]\delta^{3}(x-y)

\displaystyle\!\!+\frac{1}{1+\zeta^{2}}\left[v^{s}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.77432pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.77432pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29686pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64063pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.59366pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.56691pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{(k}}}_{{{s}{(i}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.54518pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.54518pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.61075pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2934pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.77432pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.77432pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29686pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64063pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.59366pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.56691pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{(k}}}_{{{s}{(i}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.54518pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.54518pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.61075pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2934pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.77432pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.77432pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29686pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64063pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.59366pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.56691pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{(k}}}_{{{s}{(i}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.54518pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.54518pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.61075pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2934pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.77432pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.77432pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29686pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64063pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9543pt][c]{$\textsty P}^{d}_{p}\frac{\partial\delta^{3}(x-y)}{\partial x^{d}}\,,

and

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\!\int{\rm d}^{3}z\frac{\delta{\cal T}_{ij}(x)}{\delta B^{r}_{a}(z)}\frac{\delta{\cal T}^{kl}(y)}{\delta{\cal K}^{a}_{r}(z)}

(374)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\!\qquad\quad=-\frac{1}{1+\zeta^{2}}\left(\delta_{ij}{\cal P}^{a}_{r}-\delta_{r(i}\tilde{\cal P}^{a}_{j)}\right)\delta^{r(k}\delta_{q}^{l)}K_{a}^{q}\delta^{3}(x-y)\,.

With this, the bracket (
192
) follows.

Appendix C
Time gauge

The time gauge fixes a vanishing velocity variable

v^{i}=0\,.

(375)

On the second-class constraint surface, where
\mathfrak{K}_{ij}=0
, the Lorentz constraint (
85
) can be solved for the conjugate of the velocity variable,

{\cal E}_{i}=-\partial_{a}{\cal P}^{a}_{i}\,,

(376)

and the Gauss constraint (
87
) reduces to

G_{i}=\zeta\partial_{a}{\cal
